- Expert scheduling / grouping improvements that preserve the proven BMM shape.
- Small path-isolated probes on pack/SwiGLU/unpack only if they have a measurable launch or bandwidth hypothesis.

FINAL_VERDICT

v138 is a failed experiment. Accepted correctness is not enough here: changed E64 regressed `+0.451 ms` / `+6.86%` and lowered display score to `83.33`. Roll back to v136 and exclude chunks20/24 from the next search.
